```python
def fib_1(k: int) -> int:
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(k):
        a, b = b, a + b
    return a
```

```rust
fn main() {
    let v: Vec<u32> = (0..1).collect();
    println!("{}", v.iter().sum::<u32>());
}
```
